recoveredToolResultIdsRef.current.delete(requestId);\r\n                });\r\n        });\r\n    }, [connected, snapshot, syncState]);\r\n    useEffect(() => {\r\n        confirmToolsRef.current = connectionControllerRef.current = controller;\r\n        const clientId = clientIdRef.current;\r\n        let lastEventId = \"\";\r\n        const receive = (event: LocalRuntimeEvent) => {\r\n            if (event.id) lastEventId = event.id;\r\n            if (event.type === \"hello\") {\r\n                errorLoggedRef.current = false;\r\n                connectedRef.current = true;\r\n                setAgentState({ connected: true, activity: \"已连接\", connectError: \"\", messages: useCanvasAgentStore.getState().messages.filter((item) => !isConnectionErrorMessage(item)) });\r\n }\r\n            if (event.type === \"agent_event\") {\r\n                const data = parseEventJson<AgentEventPayload>(event.data);\r\n                if (data) handleAgentEvent(data);\r\n                return;\r\n            }\r\n            if (event.type === \"agent_log\") {\r\n                const text = parseEventJson<{ text?: unknown }>(event.data)?.text;\r\n                addEventLog(\"日志\", text, text);\r\n                return;\r\n            }\r\n            if (event.type === \"agent_error\") {\r\n                const errorMessage = parseEventJson<{ message?:     setRetryMessageId(messageId || null);\r\n                }\r\n                addEventLog(\"错误\", errorMessage, errorMessage);\r\n                return;\r\n            }\r\n            if (event.type === \"agent_done\") {\r\n                setAgentState({ activity: \"完成\", waiting: false, sending: false });\r\n                void loadThreads();\r\n            }\r\n        };\r\n        void (async () => {\r\n            while (!controller.signal.aborted) {\r\n                try {\r\n                    await prepareCanvasRuntimeConnection(useLocalRuntimeStore, (controller.signal.aborted) return;\r\n                    const wasConnected = connectedRef.current;\r\n                    const text = wasConnected ? \"本地 Agent 连接已断开，正在重连\" : \"本地 Agent 连接失败，请检查本机 Runtime\";\r\n                    if (!errorLoggedRef.current || wasConnected) {\r\n                        addEventLog(wasConnected ? \"连接断开\" : \"连接失败\", text);\r\n                        if setAgentState(canvasAgentTransientDisconnectPatch(wasConnected ? \"正在重连\" : \"连接失败\", text));\r\n                    await waitForCanvasRuntimeReconnect(controller.signal);\r\n                }\r\n            }\r\n        })();\r\n        return () => {\r\n            controller.abort();\r\n            if (connectionControllerRef.current === controller) connectionControllerRef.current = null;\r\n            connectedRef.current = false;\r\n            setAgentState({ connected: false });\r\n        };\r\n    }, [enabled, loadThreads, message, setAgentState, syncState]);\r\n\r\n    useEffect(() => {\r\n        if (connected) void loadThreads();\r\n    }, [connected, loadThreads, snapshot.projectId]);\r\n\r\n    useEffect(() => {\r\n        if (activeTab === \"history\" && connected) void snapshot), 300);\r\n        return () => clearTimeout(timer);\r\n    }, [connected, snapshot, syncState]);\r\n\r\n    const submitTurn = async (payload: AgentTurnPayload, appendUserMessage: boolean) => {\r\n        const state = useCanvasAgentStore.getState();\r\n        if (!state.connected || state.sending || state.waiting) return;\r\n        activeTurnRef.current = payload;\r\n        if (appendUserMessage) {\r\n            setRetryTurn(null);\r\n            setRetryMessageId(null);\r\n        }\r\n        setAgentState({ activity: appendUserMessage ? \"发送中\" : \"重试中\", sending: true, });\r\n            addEventLog(\"用户发送\", { text: payload.text, attachments: files.map(({ name, type, size }) => ({ name, type, size })) });\r\n            // 保留附件的 data URL 供已发送消息持续显示和放大预览；仅释放 composer 的临时 object URL。\r\n            files.forEach((item) => {\r\n                URL.revokeObjectURL(item.url);\r\n                attachmentUrlsRef.current.delete(item.url);\r\n            });\r\n            setAgentState({ prompt: \"\", attachments: [] });\r\n        } else {\r\n            addEventLog(\"重试本轮\", { threadId: payload.threadId, attachments: payload.attachments.map(({ name, type }) => ({ name, type })) });\r\n        }\r\n        try {\r\n            const data = await fetchAgentJson<{ threadId?: string }>(\"/agent/codex/turn\", {\r\n                method:  }),\r\n            });\r\n            const acceptedPayload = data.threadId ? { ...payload, threadId: data.threadId } : payload;\r\n            activeTurnRef.current = acceptedPayload;\r\n            if (data.threadId) setAgentState({ activeThreadId: data.threadId });\r\n            addEventLog(appendUserMessage ? \"本地 Agent 已接收\" : \"本地 Agent 已接收重试\", { accepted: true });\r\n        } catch addEventLog(appendUserMessage ? \"发送失败\" : \"重试失败\", error);\r\n        } finally {\r\n            setAgentState({ sending: false });\r\n        }\r\n    };\r\n\r\n    const sendPrompt = async (overrideText?: string) => {\r\n        const text = (overrideText ?? prompt).trim();\r\n        const files = attachments;\r\n        if (!connected || !promptWithAttachments(text, files) || sending || waiting) return;\r\n        if (attachmentPayloadBytes(files) > MAX_ATTACHMENT_PAYLOAD_BYTES) {\r\n            addMessage({ role: \"error\", title: \"图片过大\", text: \"图片附件超过 30MB，请删减后再发送。\" });\r\n     prompt: requestPrompt,\r\n            canvasId: snapshotRef.current.projectId,\r\n            threadId: useCanvasAgentStore.getState().activeThreadId || undefined,\r\n            attachments: | null) => {\r\n        if (!files) return;\r\n        const images = Array.from(files).filter((file) => file.type.startsWith(\"image/\"));\r\n        const prev =                    return { id: createId(), name: file.name, type: file.type, size: file.size, url, dataUrl };\r\n                }),\r\n            );\r\n            const merged = [...prev, ...next];\r\n            if (attachmentPayloadBytes(merged) > MAX_ATTACHMENT_PAYLOAD_BYTES) {\r\n                next.forEach((item) => {\r\n                    URL.revokeObjectURL(item.url);\r\n                    attachmentUrlsRef.current.delete(item.url);\r\n                });\r\n                addMessage({ role: \"error\", title: \"图片过大\", text: \"图片附件最多约 30MB。\" });\r\n                return;\r\n            }\r\n            if (next.length) setAgentState({ attachments: merged });\r\n        } catch (error) {\r\n            addMessage({ role: \"error\", title: \"图片读取失败\", text: error instanceof Error ? error.message : \"图片读取失败\" });\r\n        }\r\n    };\r\n\r\n    const removeAttachment = (id: string) => {\r\n        const removed = attachments.find((item) => item.id ===    setAgentState({ pendingTool: payload, activity: \"等待确认\", waiting: false });\r\n            addEventLog(\"等待确认\", payload, payload);\r\n            return;\r\n        }\r\n        await     const input = (payload.input || {}) as Record<string, unknown>;\r\n            const projectToolName = isProjectAgentToolName(payload.name) ? payload.name : null;\r\n            setAgentState({       if (payload.name === \"canvas_apply_ops\") {\r\n                const currentSnapshot = snapshotRef.current;\r\n                if (typeof input.expectedRevision === \"number\" && input.expectedRevision !== runtimeRevisionRef.current) throw new Error(`画布 revision 已从 ${input.expectedRevision} 变为 ${runtimeRevisionRef.current}，请重新读取 canvas_get_context 后再执行写操作`);\r\n                // expectedStateHash is the Canvas Agent Runtime's canonical\r\n                // SHA-256 hash. The browser intentionally uses a different,\r\n                // synchronous fingerprint for local bookkeeping, so the\r\n                // Runtime has already performed the authoritative check before\r\n                // emitting this tool call. Comparing the two algorithms here\r\n                // would reject every valid local write.\r\n                const validation = validateCanvasAgentOps(currentSnapshot, (input.ops || []) as CanvasAgentOp[]);\r\n                if (!validation.ok) throw new Error(`画布操作校验失败：${validation.issues.filter((item) => item.severity === \"error\").map((item) => item.message).join(\"；\")}`);\r\n            }\r\n            const result =\r\n                payload.name === \"canvas_apply_ops\"\r\n                    ? snapshotRef.current\r\n                    : payload.name === \"canvas_get_context\"\r\n                      ? await readLocalCanvasContext()\r\n                      : payload.name === \"canvas_find_nodes\"\r\n                        ?    const stateHash = synced?.stateHash || runtimeCanonicalStateHashRef.current;\r\n        return buildCanvasAgentContext(snapshotRef.current, {\r\n            ...(stateHash ? { stateHash, hashSource:  pendingToolRef.current = null;\r\n        setAgentState({ pendingTool: null });\r\n        await runToolCall(tool);\r\n    };\r\n\r\n    const undoLastTool = () => {\r\n        const restored = };\r\n\r\n    useEffect(() => {\r\n        if (!autoConnect || autoConnectRef.current || enabled || connected) return;\r\n        autoConnectRef.current = true;\r\n        void };\r\n\r\n    const resumeThread = async (threadId: string) => {\r\n        const projectId = snapshotRef.current.projectId;\r\n        if (!connected || !projectId || !threadId) return;\r\n        setAgentState({ loadingThreads: true });\r\n        try {\r\n            const data = await fetchAgentJson<AgentThreadResponse>(`/agent/codex/threads/${encodeURIComponent(threadId)}/resume`, { method: fetchAgentJson(`/agent/codex/threads/${encodeURIComponent(threadId)}/delete`, { method: \"POST\", headers: { \"content-type\": \"application/json\" }, body: JSON.stringify({ canvasId: projectId }) });\r\n            const current = useCanvasAgentStore.getState();\r\n            if (current.activeThreadId === threadId) {\r\n                activeTurnRef.current = null;\r\n                setRetryTurn(null);\r\n                setRetryMessageId(null);\r\n            }\r\n            setAgentState({\r\n                threads: current.threads.filter((thread) => thread.id !== threadId),\r\n                activeThreadId: current.activeThreadId === threadId ? \"\" : current.activeThreadId,\r\n                messages: current.activeThreadId === threadId ? [] : current.messages,\r\n            });\r\n            message.success(\"记录已删除\");\r\n        } catch (error) {\r\n            addEventLog(\"删除对话失败\", error);\r\n            message.error(error instanceof Error ? error.message : \"删除对话失败\");\r\n        } finally {\r\n            setAgentState({ loadingThreads: false });\r\n        }\r\n    };\r\n\r\n    const confirmDeleteThread = (thread: AgentThreadSummary) => {\r\n        const label = thread.name || thread.preview || \"未命名对话\";\r\n        modal.confirm({\r\n            title: \"删除对话记录\",\r\n            content: `确定删除「${label.length > 48 ? `${label.slice(0, 48)}...` : label}」吗？`,\r\n            okText: \"删除\",\r\n            okType: \"danger\",\r\n            cancelText: \"取消\",\r\n            onOk: () => deleteThread(thread.id),\r\n        });\r\n    };\r\n\r\n    const startResize = (event: ReactPointerEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {\r\n        event.preventDefault();\r\n        const startX = event.clientX;\r\n        const startWidth = width;\r\n        let nextWidth = startWidth;\r\n        const onMove = (moveEvent: PointerEvent) => {\r\n            nextWidth = clamp(startWidth + startX - moveEvent.clientX, 360, 760);\r\n            setAgentState({ width: nextWidth });\r\n        };\r\n        const onUp = () => {\r\n            localStorage.setItem(\"canvas-agent-panel-width\", String(nextWidth));\r\n            window.removeEventListener(\"pointermove\", onMove);\r\n            window.removeEventListener(\"pointerup\", onUp);\r\n            setResizing(false);\r\n        };\r\n        setResizing(true);\r\n        window.addEventListener(\"pointermove\", onMove);\r\n        window.addEventListener(\"pointerup\", onUp);\r\n    };\r\n\r\n    const addMessage = (item: Omit<AgentChatItem, \"id\">) => {\r\n        const text = normalizeText(item.text);\r\n        if (!text && !item.attachments?.length) return undefined;\r\n        const next = { ...item, id: `${Date.now()}-${Math.random()}`, text };\r\n        const currentMessages = useCanvasAgentStore.getState().messages;\r\n        if (next.streamId) {\r\n            const index = currentMessages.findIndex((message) => message.streamId === next.streamId);\r\n            if (index >= 0) {\r\n                setAgentState({ messages: currentMessages.map((message, i) => (i === index ? { ...message, ...next, id: message.id, text: next.text || message.text } : message)) });\r\n                return currentMessages[index].id;\r\n            }\r\n        }\r\n        const last = currentMessages.at(-1);\r\n        if (last?.role === \"assistant\" && next.role === \"assistant\" && last.title === next.title) {\r\n            const merged = mergeAgentText(last.text, next.text);\r\n            if (merged === last.text) return last.id;\r\n            setAgentState({ messages: [...useCanvasAgentStore.getState().messages.slice(0, -1), { ...last, text: merged, meta: next.meta || last.meta }] });\r\n            return last.id;\r\n        }\r\n        pushMessage(next);\r\n        return next.id;\r\n    };\r\n\r\n    const addEventLog = (title: string, text: unknown, raw?: unknown) => {\r\n        pushEventLog({ id: `${Date.now()}-${Math.random()}`, time: new Date().toLocaleTimeString(), title, text: normalizeText(text) || title, raw });\r\n    };\r\n\r\n    const handleAgentEvent = (event: AgentEventPayload) => {\r\n        if (shouldLogAgentEvent(event)) addEventLog(eventTitle(event), event, event);\r\n        if (event.type === \"thread.started\" && event.thread_id) setAgentState({ activeThreadId: event.thread_id });\r\n        const nextActivity = activityText(event);\r\n        if (nextActivity) setAgentState({ activity: nextActivity });\r\n        if (event.type === \"turn.started\") setAgentState({ waiting: true });\r\n        if (event.type === \"turn.completed\") {\r\n            activeTurnRef.current = null;\r\n            setRetryTurn(null);\r\n            setRetryMessageId(null);\r\n            setAgentState({ waiting: false, sending: false });\r\n        } else if (event.type === \"turn.failed\" || event.type === \"error\") {\r\n            if (activeTurnRef.current) setRetryTurn(activeTurnRef.current);\r\n            setAgentState({ waiting: false, sending: false });\r\n        }\r\n        const item = formatAgentEvent(event);\r\n        if (item) {\r\n            if (item.role === \"error\") setAgentState({ waiting: false, sending: false });\r\n            const messageId = addMessage(item);\r\n            if ((event.type === \"turn.failed\" || event.type === \"error\") && activeTurnRef.current && item.role === \"error\") setRetryMessageId(messageId || null);\r\n        }\r\n    };\r\n\r\n    const content = (\r\n        <>\r\n            <div className=\"flex min-h-8 shrink-0 items-center justify-end gap-1 px-3 pb-1\">\r\n                <div className=\"mr-auto min-w-0 truncate px-1 activity, connectError })}\r\n                </div>\r\n                {!connected ? (\r\n                    <Button size=\"small\" type={enabled ? \"default\" : \"primary\"} className=\"!h-7 !px-2.5\" icon={<PlugZap className=\"size-3.5\" />} onClick={toggleAgentConnection}>\r\n                        {enabled ? \"连接中\" : \"连接\"}\r\n                    </Button>\r\n                ) : null}\r\n                <Tooltip title={threads.length ? `历史会话 · ${threads.length}` : \"历史会话\"}>\r\n                    <Button type=\"text\" className={`!h-7 !min-w-7 !px-1.5 ${activeTab === \"history\" ? \"font-medium\" : \"\"}`} style={{ color: activeTab === \"history\" ? theme.node.text : theme.node.muted, background: activeTab === \"history\" ? theme.spatial.surface : \"transparent\" }} icon={<History className=\"size-3.5\" />} onClick={() => setAgentState({ activeTab: activeTab === \"history\" ? \"chat\" : \"history\" })} aria-label=\"打开历史会话\">\r\n                        {threads.length ? <span className=\"text-[var(--fs-tiny)] tabular-nums\">{threads.length}</span> : null}\r\n                    </Button>\r\n                </Tooltip>\r\n                <Tooltip title=\"新对话\">\r\n                    <Button type=\"text\" shape=\"circle\" className=\"!h-7 !w-7 !min-w-7\" disabled={!connected || loadingThreads} style={{ color: theme.node.muted }} icon={<Plus className=\"size-3.5\" />} onClick={() => void startNewThread()} aria-label=\"新建对话\" />\r\n                </Tooltip>\r\n            </div>\r\n\r\n            {!connected ?            }\r\n                    />\r\n                </>\r\n            )}\r\n        </>\r\n    );\r\n\r\n    if (headless) return null;\r\n    if (embedded) return content;\r\n\r\n    return AgentLogView({\r\n    logs,\r\n    theme,\r\n    context,\r\n    onClear,\r\n    onCopied,\r\n    onCopyBlocked,\r\n}: {\r\n    logs: AgentEventLog[];\r\n    theme: (typeof canvasThemes)[keyof typeof context);\r\n    const lastError = [...logs].reverse().find((item) => /错误|失败|error/i.test(`${item.title}\\n${item.text}`));\r\n    const copy = async (value = content, tip = \"日志已复制\") => {\r\n            <Button size=\"small\" danger type=\"text\" icon={<Trash2 className=\"size-3.5\" />} disabled={!logs.length} onClick={onClear}>\r\n                            清空\r\n {connectError}\r\n                            </div>\r\n                        ) : null}\r\n                    </div>\r\n                </div>\r\n\r\n                <div>\r\n        </div>\r\n    );\r\n}\r\n\r\nfunction AgentHistoryView({\r\n    theme,\r\n    threads,\r\n    activeThreadId,\r\n    workspacePath,\r\n    loading,\r\n    connected,\r\n    onRefresh,\r\n   { value: \"1:1\", label: \"方形\" },\r\n    { value: \"16:9\", label: \"横屏\" },\r\n    { value: \"9:16\", label: \"竖屏\" },\r\n    { value: \"4:3\", label: \"标准横屏\" },\r\n    { value: \"3:4\", label:      shots.push({ result: message });\r\n        }\r\n    }\r\n    return shots;\r\n}\r\n\r\nfunction completedCreationGenerationTask(input: { taskId: string; task?: GenerationTask; mode: \"image\" |  return projectGenerationTaskResult({ ...task, status: \"succeeded\", prompt: input.prompt, clientContext: { conversationId: input.conversationId, messageId: input.messageId, ...(typeof };\r\n\r\n    const selectConversation = (conversation: CreationConversation) => {\r\n        if (draftReplacementBlocked()) return;\r\n        draftEpochRef.current += 1;\r\n setSelectedShotIndex(-1);\r\n        setComposingNextShot(false);\r\n        setHistoryOpen(false);\r\n    };\r\n\r\n    const confirmDeleteConversation = (conversation: CreationConversation) => {\r\n        const title = conversation.title.trim() || \"新创作\";\r\n        const label = title.length > 32 ? `${title.slice(0, 32)}...` : title;\r\n        modal.confirm({\r\n            className: \"workspace-modal workspace-modal-compact\",\r\n            title: \"删除历史对话？\",\r\n            content: `确定删除「${label}」吗？这只会删除历史对话记录，不会删除已上传或生成的任何素材。此操作不可撤销。`,\r\n            okText: \"删除对话\",\r\n            okButtonProps: { danger: true },\r\n            cancelText: \"保留\",\r\n            onOk: async () => {\r\n                if (draftReplacementBlocked()) throw new Error(\"当前创作内容仍在准备，请稍后再删除对话。\");\r\n                try {\r\n                    const remaining = removeCreationConversationSnapshot(conversationsRef.current, conversation.id);\r\n                    const sortedRemaining = [...remaining].sort((left, right) => conversationTimestamp(right.updatedAt) - conversationTimestamp(left.updatedAt));\r\n                    const fallback = sortedRemaining.find((item) => item.messages.length > 0) || sortedRemaining[0] || newConversation();\r\n                    const next = remaining.length ? remaining : [fallback];\r\n                    await saveCreationConversations(next);\r\n                    conversationsRef.current = next;\r\n                    setConversations(next);\r\n                    if (activeIdRef.current === conversation.id) {\r\n                        draftEpochRef.current += 1;\r\n                        followLatestMessageRef.current = true;\r\n                        activeIdRef.current = fallback.id;\r\n                        setActiveId(fallback.id);\r\n                        setPrompt(\"\");\r\n                        setAttachments([]);\r\n                        setDraftReferences([]);\r\n                        setSelectedShotIndex(-1);\r\n                        setComposingNextShot(false);\r\n                    }\r\n                    toast.success(\"历史对话已删除，素材仍保留\");\r\n                } catch (error) {\r\n                    toast.error(error instanceof Error ? error.message : \"历史对话删除失败\");\r\n                    throw error;\r\n                }\r\n            },\r\n        });\r\n    };\r\n\r\n    const restoreMessageDraft = (item: CreationMessage) => {\r\n        draftEpochRef.current += 1;\r\n        const nextMode = item.mode || \"text\";\r\n        const nextSettings = item.settings;\r\n        setAgentIntent(false);\r\n        setMode(nextMode);\r\n        setPrompt(item.content);\r\n        setAttachments(item.attachments ? [...item.attachments] : []);\r\n        setDraftReferences(item.references ? [...item.references] : []);\r\n        if (item.model) updateConfig(nextMode === \"text\" ? \"textModel\" : nextMode === \"image\" ? \"imageModel\" : \"videoModel\", item.model);\r\n        if (!nextSettings) return;\r\n        setRatio(nextSettings.ratio);\r\n        setSeconds(nextSettings.seconds);\r\n        setQuality(nextSettings.quality);\r\n        setVideoQuality(nextSettings.videoQuality);\r\n        setCount(nextSettings.count);\r\n    };\r\n\r\n    const retryFailedMessage = async (item: CreationMessage, index: number) => {\r\n        const previous = item.role === \"assistant\" ? activeConversation?.messages[index - 1] : item;\r\n        if (!previous?.content || busy || draftReplacementBlocked()) return;\r\n        const retryOf = item.taskIds?.[0];\r\n        const restoreForRetry = () => {\r\n            followLatestMessageRef.current = true;\r\n            restoreMessageDraft(previous);\r\n            setSelectedShotIndex(-1);\r\n            setComposingNextShot(false);\r\n            const removedIds = new Set([item.id, previous.id]);\r\n            updateActive((conversation) => {\r\n                const messages = conversation.messages.filter((message) => !removedIds.has(message.id));\r\n                const firstPrompt = messages.find((message) => message.role === \"user\")?.content.trim();\r\n                return { ...conversation, title: firstPrompt ? firstPrompt.slice(0, 24) : \"新创作\", updatedAt: new Date().toISOString(), messages };\r\n            });\r\n        };\r\n        if (!retryOf) {\r\n            restoreForRetry();\r\n            return;\r\n        }\r\n        if (retryPreparingRef.current.has(retryOf)) return;\r\n        retryPreparingRef.current.add(retryOf);\r\n        preparingRef.current = true;\r\n        setPreparing(true);\r\n        const scope = getActiveUserScope();\r\n        const conversationId = activeIdRef.current;\r\n        try {\r\n            const attemptGroupId = item.attemptGroupId || item.retryOf || retryOf;\r\n            const context: CreationRetryContext = { ...(await createGenerationRetryContext(retryOf, attemptGroupId)), ...(item.taskIds && item.taskIds.length > 1 ? { retryContextsByBatchIndex: await createGenerationBatchRetryContexts(item.taskIds, attemptGroupId) } : {}) };\r\n            if (scope !== getActiveUserScope() || conversationId !== activeIdRef.current) throw new Error(\"账号或对话已变化，请重新选择需要重试的作品。\");\r\n            restoreForRetry();\r\n            pendingRetryRef.current = { context, lockKey: retryOf };\r\n            setRetrySequence((current) => current + 1);\r\n        } catch (error) {\r\n            retryPreparingRef.current.delete(retryOf);\r\n            toast.error(generationErrorMessage(error));\r\n        } finally { preparingRef.current = false; setPreparing(false); }\r\n    };\r\n\r\n    const createVariant = (item: CreationMessage, index: number) => {\r\n        const previous = item.role === \"assistant\" ? activeConversation?.messages[index - 1] : item;\r\n        if (!previous?.content || busy || draftReplacementBlocked()) return;\r\n        restoreMessageDraft(previous);\r\n    };\r\n\r\n    if (!hydrated || !activeConversation) return <div className=\"grid h-full place-items-center\"><Spin /></div>;\r\n\r\n    const handleThreadScroll = () => {\r\n        const container = threadScrollRef.current;\r\n        if (!container) return;\r\n        followLatestMessageRef.current = container.scrollHeight - container.scrollTop - container.clientHeight <= 160;\r\n    };\r\n\r\n    const nextShotNumber = shots.length + 1;\r\n\r\n    const beginComposeNextShot = () => {\r\n        setComposingNextShot(true);\r\n        setSelectedShotIndex(-1);\r\n        window.requestAnimationFrame(() => composerFocusRef.current?.focus());\r\n    };\r\n\r\n    const cancelComposeNextShot = () => setComposingNextShot(false);\r\n\r\n    const submitLobbyToCanvas = async () => {\r\n        const text = prompt.trim();\r\n        if (submissionBlocked || uploadingRef.current || skillLoadsRef.current > 0) {\r\n            toast.warning(inputIssues[0] || (uploadFailures.length ? \"请重试或移除失败的附件后再继续。\" : \"附件或技能正在加载，请稍候。\"));\r\n            return;\r\n        }\r\n        const missingSkill = [...text.matchAll(/@\\[skill:([^\\]]+)\\]/g)].some(match => !mentionReferences.some(reference => reference.skill?.skillId === match[1] && reference.skill.instruction));\r\n        if (missingSkill) {\r\n            toast.warning(\"专业技能尚未加载完整，请稍后重试或重新选择技能。\");\r\n            return;\r\n        }\r\n        if (!text || busy || preparingRef.current || agentLaunch.busy || retryPreparingRef.current.size > 0) return;\r\n        const skills = selectedCreationReferences(text, mentionReferences).flatMap(reference => reference.skill ? [reference.skill] : []);\r\n        try {\r\n            await agentLaunch.launch({\r\n                prompt: text,\r\n                attachments,\r\n                skills,\r\n                models: { text: config.textModel, image: config.imageModel, video: config.videoModel },\r\n                confirmTools,\r\n                entryMode: agentIntent ? \"agent\" : mode,\r\n                requireTextModel: false,\r\n            });\r\n        } catch (reason) {\r\n            toast.error(reason instanceof Error ? reason.message : \"创作草稿未能交给映雪画布，输入内容已保留。\");\r\n        }\r\n    };\r\n\r\n    const composerProps = {\r\n        agentIntent,\r\n        onAgentIntentChange: () => { if (!preparingRef.current && !agentLaunch.busy) { setAgentIntent(true); setMode(\"text\"); } },\r\n        confirmTools,\r\n        onConfirmToolsChange: (confirmTools: boolean) => setAgentState({ confirmTools }),\r\n        onSelectSkill: selectSkill,\r\n        uploading,\r\n        uploadFailures,\r\n        inputIssues,\r\n        submissionBlocked,\r\n        onAddFiles: (files: File[]) => void addAttachments(files),\r\n        onRetryUploads: () => void addAttachments(uploadFailures.map(item => item.file)),\r\n        onDismissUploadFailure: (file: File) => setUploadFailures(current => current.filter(item => item.file !== file)),\r\n        mode,\r\n        prompt,\r\n        setPrompt: (value: string) => { if (!preparingRef.current && !agentLaunch.busy) setPrompt(value); },\r\n        busy: busy || agentLaunch.busy || preparing,\r\n        attachments,\r\n        referenceImageSize,\r\n        maxReferences,\r\n        references: mentionReferences,\r\n        onRemoveAttachment: removeAttachment,\r\n        onReorderAttachments: reorderAttachments,\r\n        onOpenLibrary: () => setLibraryOpen(true),\r\n        fileInputRef,\r\n        onFileChange: handleFileChange,\r\n        onModeChange: selectMode,\r\n        model: selectedModel,\r\n        modelRequirements,\r\n        imageProfile,\r\n        videoProfile,\r\n        config,\r\n        onModelChange: (value: string) => updateConfig(agentIntent || mode === \"text\" ? \"textModel\" : mode === \"image\" ? \"imageModel\" : \"videoModel\", value),\r\n        onAgentModelChange: (kind: \"text\" | \"image\" | \"video\", value: string) => updateConfig(kind === \"text\" ? \"textModel\" : kind === \"image\" ? \"imageModel\" : \"videoModel\", value),\r\n        ratio,\r\n        setRatio,\r\n        seconds,\r\n        setSeconds,\r\n        quality,\r\n        setQuality,\r\n        videoQuality,\r\n        setVideoQuality,\r\n        count,\r\n        setCount,\r\n        promptOptimizerProvider,\r\n        composerFocusRef,\r\n        placeholderOverride: viewMode === \"storyboard\" && composingNextShot ? `SC.${String(nextShotNumber).padStart(2, \"0\")} · 写下这一镜的镜头、画面或故事` : undefined,\r\n        onSubmit: () => void submit(),\r\n    };\r\n\r\n    const visibleShot = shots[visibleShotIndex];\r\n    const visibleShotResultIndex = visibleShot?.result ? activeConversation.messages.indexOf(visibleShot.result) : -1;\r\n\r\n    const workspace = <div className=\"creation-home relative flex h-full min-h-0 flex-col overflow-hidden\">\r\n            {isEmpty ? <>\r\n                <div className=\"creation-top-actions\">\r\n                    <Tooltip title=\"历史对话\"><button type=\"button\" aria-label=\"查看历史对话\" aria-expanded={historyOpen} className=\"creation-top-action\" onClick={() => setHistoryOpen(true)}><History /></button></Tooltip>\r\n                </div>\r\n                <main ref={threadScrollRef} onScroll={handleThreadScroll} className=\"creation-empty-workspace creation-scrollbar\">\r\n                <CreationEmptyBanner />\r\n                <div className=\"creation-chat-intro\">\r\n                    <span className=\"creation-intro-signal\" aria-hidden=\"true\" />\r\n                    <p>映雪 · AI 创作工作台</p>\r\n                    <h1>让你的下一个想法，<span className=\"creation-intro-emphasis\"><span className=\"is-pink\">在这里</span><span className=\"is-blue\">成为作品</span></span></h1>\r\n                </div>\r\n                <div className=\"creation-empty-composer\">\r\n                    <CreationComposer {...composerProps} variant=\"empty\" />\r\n                </div>\r\n                <CreationEmptySuggest\r\n                    onStartPrompt={(nextMode, prompt) => { selectMode(nextMode); setPrompt(prompt); window.requestAnimationFrame(() => composerFocusRef.current?.focus()); }}\r\n                    onOpenLibrary={() => { selectMode(\"image\"); setLibraryOpen(true); }}\r\n                />\r\n            </main>\r\n            </> : viewMode === \"chat\" ? [item.id.slice(6)] : item.id.startsWith(\"external:\") ? [item.id] : [])}\r\n            upload={{ accept: creationUploadAccept(agentIntent ? \"text\" : mode), description: agentIntent || mode === \"text\" ? \"支持图片、视频、音频和常用文档；媒体会保存到素材库\" : `支持图片${mode === \"video\" ? \"、视频和音频\" : \"\"}，上传后保存到素材库`, onUpload: uploadLibraryAssets, external: { accept: \"image/*\", description: \"写入当前 Eagle 文件夹；Eagle 当前支持图片文件\", onUpload: (files, folderId) => externalAssetSources.uploadExternalFiles(files, folderId) } }}\r\n            onClose={() => setLibraryOpen(false)}\r\n            onConfirm={handleLibrarySelect}\r\n        />\r\n    </>;\r\n}\r\n\r\nconst creationAssetCategoryLabels: Record<string, string> = { all: \"全部素材\", character: \"角色\", environment: \"场景\", wardrobe: \"服饰\", prop: \"道具\", weapon: \"武器\", style: \"画风\", other: \"其他\" };\r\n\r\nfunction CreationHistoryDrawer({ open, conversations, activeId, onClose, onSelect, onDelete }: { open: boolean; conversations: CreationConversation[]; activeId: string; onClose: () => void; onSelect: (conversation: CreationConversation) => void; onDelete: (conversation: CreationConversation) => void }) {\r\n    const [keyword, setKeyword] = useState(\"\");\r\n\r\n    useEffect(() => {\r\n        if (open) setKeyword(\"\");\r\n    }, [open]);\r\n\r\n    const visibleConversations = useMemo(() => {\r\n        const query = keyword.trim().toLowerCase();\r\n        if (!query) return conversations;\r\n        return conversations.filter((conversation) => {\r\n            const latest = conversationPreviewMessage(conversation);\r\n            const searchable = [\r\n                conversation.title,\r\n                ...conversation.messages.flatMap((message) => [message.content, displayCreationPrompt(message.content, message.references || [])]),\r\n                latest?.mode ? modeLabels[latest.mode] : \"创作\",\r\n                formatConversationTime(conversation.updatedAt),\r\n            ].filter(Boolean).join(\" \").toLowerCase();\r\n            return searchable.includes(query);\r\n        });\r\n    }, [conversations, keyword]);\r\n\r\n    return <Drawer open={open} onClose={onClose} placement=\"right\" size=\"min(440px, 100vw)\" closeIcon={<X className=\"size-4\" />} className=\"creation-history-drawer\" rootClassName=\"creation-history-drawer-root\" styles={{ body: { padding: 0 } }} title={<div className=\"creation-history-title\"><span>历史对话</span><small>{conversations.length} 个对话</small></div>}>\r\n        <div className=\"creation-history-content\">\r\n            <label className=\"creation-history-search\">\r\n                <Search aria-hidden=\"true\" />\r\n                <input value={keyword} onChange={(event) => setKeyword(event.target.value)} placeholder=\"搜索对话标题或内容\" aria-label=\"搜索历史对话\" />\r\n            </label>\r\n            {visibleConversations.length ? <ul className=\"creation-history-list\" aria-label=\"历史对话，按更新时间倒序排列\">\r\n                {visibleConversations.map((conversation) => {\r\n                    const latest = conversationPreviewMessage(conversation);\r\n                    const active = conversation.id === activeId;\r\n                    return <li key={conversation.id} className={active ? \"is-active\" : undefined}>\r\n                        <button type=\"button\" className=\"creation-history-item-main\" aria-current={active ? \"page\" : undefined} onClick={() => onSelect(conversation)}>\r\n                            <span className=\"creation-history-time\"><time dateTime={conversation.updatedAt}>{formatConversationTime(conversation.updatedAt)}</time><em>{latest?.mode ? modeLabels[latest.mode] : \"创作\"}</em></span>\r\n                            <strong className=\"creation-history-item-heading\">{conversation.title.trim() || \"新创作\"}</strong>\r\n                            <span className=\"creation-history-snippet\">{latest ? displayCreationPrompt(latest.content, latest.references || []).trim() || \"还没有开始创作\" : \"还没有开始创作\"}</span>\r\n                        </button>\r\n                        <Tooltip title=\"删除对话\"><button type=\"button\" className=\"creation-history-delete\" aria-label={`删除对话：${conversation.title.trim() || \"新创作\"}`} onClick={() => onDelete(conversation)}><Trash2 /></button></Tooltip>\r\n                    </li>;\r\n                })}\r\n            </ul> : <div className=\"creation-history-empty\">{keyword.trim() ? \"没有找到匹配的对话\" : \"暂无历史对话\"}</div>}\r\n        </div>\r\n    </Drawer>;\r\n}\r\n\r\nfunction CreationViewSwitch({ viewMode, onChange }: { viewMode: CreationViewMode; onChange: (mode: CreationViewMode) => void }) {\r\n    return <div className=\"creation-view-switch\" role=\"group\" aria-label=\"创作视图\">\r\n        <button type=\"button\" aria-pressed={viewMode === \"chat\"} onClick={() => onChange(\"chat\")}><MessageSquareText />连续对话</button>\r\n        <button type=\"button\" aria-pressed={viewMode === \"storyboard\"} onClick={() => onChange(\"storyboard\")}><Clapperboard />镜头创作</button>\r\n    </div>;\r\n}\r\n\r\nfunction CreationWorkspaceToolbar({ viewMode, onViewModeChange, onNewConversation, onOpenHistory }: { viewMode: CreationViewMode; onViewModeChange: (mode: CreationViewMode) => void; onNewConversation: () => void; onOpenHistory: () => void }) {\r\n    return <header className=\"creation-thread-toolbar\">\r\n        <CreationViewSwitch viewMode={viewMode} onChange={onViewModeChange} />\r\n        <div className=\"storyboard-workbench-bar-actions\">\r\n            <Tooltip title=\"新建创作\"><button type=\"button\" aria-label=\"新建创作\" className=\"storyboard-workbench-bar-action\" onClick={onNewConversation}><Plus /></button></Tooltip>\r\n            <Tooltip title=\"历史对话\"><button type=\"button\" aria-label=\"查看历史对话\" className=\"storyboard-workbench-bar-action\" onClick={onOpenHistory}><History /></button></Tooltip>\r\n        </div>\r\n    </header>;\r\n}\r\n\r\nfunction CreationMessageView({ item, modelName, onRetryFailure, onCreateVariant }: { item: CreationMessage; modelName: string; onRetryFailure: () => void; onCreateVariant: () => void }) {\r\n    if (item.role === \"user\") return <CreationUserMessage item={item} />;\r\n    const mode = item.mode || \"text\";\r\n    const stateLabel = item.status === \"pending\" ? \"生成中\" : item.status === \"cancelled\" ? \"已停止\" : item.status === \"error\" ? \"生成失败\" : \"\";\r\n    const heading = <><span className=\"creation-message-mark\"><Sparkles /></span><strong>{mode === \"image\" ? \"图像生成\" : mode === \"video\" ? \"视频生成\" : \"映雪 AI\"}</strong>{mode !== \"text\" ? <span className=\"creation-message-progress-copy\">{item.status === \"pending\" ? `映雪正在生成${mode === \"video\" ? \"视频\" : \"图像\"}……` : item.status === \"done\" ? `你的${mode === \"video\" ? \"视频\" : \"图像\"}已创建` : null}</span> : null}{modelName ? <span className=\"creation-message-model\">{modelName}</span> : null}{item.createdAt ? <time dateTime={item.createdAt}>{formatMessageTime(item.createdAt)}</time> : null}{stateLabel ? <span className={`creation-message-state is-${item.status}`}>{stateLabel}</span> : null}</>;\r\n    const toolStatus: GenerationToolStatus = item.status === \"pending\" ? \"running\" : item.status === \"error\" ? \"error\" : item.status === \"cancelled\" ? \"cancelled\" : \"completed\";\r\n    return <article className={`creation-assistant-message is-${mode}`}>\r\n        {mode === \"text\" ? <><div className=\"creation-message-heading\">{heading}</div>{item.reasoning ? <MessageReasoning reasoning={item.reasoning} isStreaming={item.status === \"streaming\"} /> : null}<div className=\"creation-message-content\">{item.content ? <AIMessageMarkdown isStreaming={item.status === \"streaming\"}>{item.content}</AIMessageMarkdown> : <span>正在生成…</span>}</div></> : <GenerationToolCard status={toolStatus} heading={heading}><MediaResult item={item} onRetryFailure={onRetryFailure} onCreateVariant={onCreateVariant} /></GenerationToolCard>}\r\n        {item.error && mode === \"text\" ? <div className=\"creation-message-error\"><span>{generationErrorMessage(item.error)}</span><button type=\"button\" onClick={onRetryFailure}><RefreshCw />重新生成</button></div> : null}\r\n    </article>;\r\n}\r\n\r\nfunction CreationUserMessage({ item }: { item: CreationMessage }) {\r\n    const [previewUrl, setPreviewUrl] = useState(\"\");\r\n    const [previewType, setPreviewType] = useState<\"image\" | \"video\">(\"image\");\r\n    const copyText = useCopyText();\r\n    const visiblePrompt = displayCreationPrompt(item.content, item.references || []);\r\n    return <article className=\"creation-user-message\">\r\n        <div className=\"creation-user-message-meta\"><span>你</span>{item.createdAt ? <time dateTime={item.createdAt}>{formatMessageTime(item.createdAt)}</time> : null}<Tooltip title=\"复制消息\"><button type=\"button\" className=\"creation-user-message-copy\" aria-label=\"复制提示词\" onClick={() => copyText(visiblePrompt, \"提示词已复制\")}><Copy /></button></Tooltip></div>\r\n        <div className=\"creation-user-message-copy-wrap\"><p>{visiblePrompt}</p></div>\r\n        {item.references?.length ? <CreationMessageReferences references={item.references} /> : null}\r\n        {item.attachments?.length ? <div className=\"creation-user-message-attachments\">{item.attachments.map((attachment) => {\r\n            const kind = creationAttachmentKind(attachment);\r\n            const previewable = kind === \"image\" || kind === \"video\";\r\n            const url = attachment.previewUrl || (\"dataUrl\" in attachment ? attachment.dataUrl : attachment.url) || \"\";\r\n            return <button key={attachment.id} type=\"button\" className={!previewable ? \"is-file\" : undefined} onClick={() => { if (!previewable) return; setPreviewType(kind === \"video\" ? \"video\" : \"image\"); setPreviewUrl(kind === \"video\" ? attachment.url || \"\" : url); }} aria-label={previewable ? `预览 ${attachment.name || \"附件\"}` : attachment.name || \"附件\"} disabled={previewable && !url}>{kind === \"video\" ? <video src={attachment.url || \"\"} poster={url !== attachment.url ? url : undefined} muted playsInline preload=\"metadata\" /> : kind === \"image\" ? <img src={url} alt={attachment.name || \"附件\"} width={44} height={44} loading=\"lazy\" /> : kind === \"audio\" ? <Music2 /> : <FileText />}{previewable ? <span aria-hidden=\"true\"><Maximize2 /></span> : null}</button>;\r\n        })}</div> : null}\r\n        <CreationMediaPreviewModal url={previewUrl} type={previewType} onClose={() => setPreviewUrl(\"\")} />\r\n    </article>;\r\n}\r\n\r\nfunction MediaResult({ item, onRetryFailure, onCreateVariant }: { item: CreationMessage; onRetryFailure: () => void; onCreateVariant: () => void }) {\r\n    const [previewUrl, setPreviewUrl] = useState(\"\");\r\n    const [previewType, setPreviewType] = useState<\"image\" | \"video\">(\"image\");\r\n    const assets = useAssetStore((state) => state.assets);\r\n    const resultUrls = item.resultUrls || [];\r\n    const resultAssetIds = resultUrls.length ? creationResultAssetIds(assets, { messageId: item.id, taskIds: item.taskIds || [], resultUrls }) : [];\r\n    const canvasPath = creationCanvasHandoffPath(resultAssetIds) || \"/canvas\";\r\n    if (item.status === \"pending\") return <CreationMediaPending mode={item.mode || \"image\"} ratio={item.settings?.ratio} />;\r\n    if ((item.status === \"error\" || item.status === \"cancelled\") && !resultUrls.length) return <div className=\"creation-media-error\"><span>{item.status === \"cancelled\" ? item.content || \"已停止\" : generationErrorMessage(item.error || \"生成失败\")}</span><button type=\"button\" onClick={onRetryFailure}><RefreshCw />重新生成</button></div>;\r\n    if (!resultUrls.length) return <div className=\"creation-media-empty\">没有返回可预览结果 <button type=\"button\" onClick={onRetryFailure}>重试</button></div>;\r\n    const isVideo = item.mode === \"video\";\r\n    return <div className=\"creation-media-result\">\r\n        {isVideo ? <button type=\"button\" className=\"creation-video-result\" onClick={() => { setPreviewType(\"video\"); setPreviewUrl(resultUrls[0]); }} aria-label=\"预览生成视频\"><video muted preload=\"metadata\" src={resultUrls[0]} /><span><Maximize2 />预览视频</span></button> : <div className=\"creation-image-result-grid\">{resultUrls.map((url) => <button key={url} type=\"button\" className=\"creation-image-result\" onClick={() => { setPreviewType(\"image\"); setPreviewUrl(url); }} aria-label=\"预览生成图片\"><img src={url} alt=\"生成结果\" /><span><Maximize2 /></span></button>)}</div>}\r\n        <div className=\"creation-media-actions\"><span>{isVideo ? \"视频结果\" : `${resultUrls.length} 张图片`}</span><button type=\"button\" onClick={onCreateVariant}><RefreshCw />生成同款</button><Link to={canvasPath}>{resultAssetIds.length ? \"添加到画布\" : \"打开画布\"}</Link>{resultUrls.map((url, index) => <a key={`${url}-download`} href={url} download>{resultUrls.length > 1 ? `下载 ${index + 1}` : <><Download />下载</>}</a>)}</div>\r\n        <CreationMediaPreviewModal url={previewUrl} type={previewType} onClose={() => setPreviewUrl(\"\")} />\r\n    </div>;\r\n}\r\n\r\nfunction CreationMediaPending({ mode, ratio }: { mode: CreationMode; ratio?: string }) {\r\n    return <div className={`creation-media-pending is-${mode}`} style={{ aspectRatio: creationMediaAspectRatio(ratio, mode) }} aria-live=\"polite\"><span className=\"creation-media-pending-icon\"><Sparkles /></span><span className=\"sr-only\">映雪正在生成{mode === \"video\" ? \"视频\" : \"图像\"}</span></div>;\r\n}\r\n\r\nfunction CreationMessageReferences({ references }: { references: CreationReference[] }) {\r\n    return <div className=\"creation-user-message-references\" aria-label=\"本次引用\">{references.map((reference) => {\r\n        const Icon = reference.kind === \"skill\" ? Sparkles : reference.kind === \"image\" ? ImageIcon : reference.kind === \"video\" ? Film : reference.kind === \"audio\" ? Music2 : FileText;\r\n        return <span key={reference.id} className=\"creation-user-message-reference\">{reference.previewUrl && reference.kind === \"video\" ? <video src={reference.previewUrl} muted playsInline preload=\"metadata\" aria-label={reference.label} /> : reference.previewUrl && reference.kind === \"image\" ? <img src={reference.previewUrl} alt=\"\" /> : <Icon />}<span>{reference.label}</span></span>;\r\n    })}</div>;\r\n}\r\n\r\nfunction CreationMediaPreviewModal({ url, type, onClose }: { url: string; type: \"image\" | \"video\" | \"audio\"; onClose: () => void }) {\r\n    return <Modal open={Boolean(url)} title={null} footer={null} centered destroyOnHidden width={type === \"video\" ? \"min(1160px, calc(100vw - 32px))\" : type === \"audio\" ? \"min(560px, calc(100vw - 32px))\" : \"min(980px, calc(100vw - 32px))\"} onCancel={onClose} className=\"creation-media-preview-modal\" styles={{ body: { padding: 0 } }}>{url ? type === \"video\" ? <video controls autoPlay className=\"creation-media-preview-video\" src={url} /> : type === \"audio\" ? <div className=\"creation-audio-preview\"><Music2 size={36} /><audio controls src={url} aria-label=\"参考音频试听\" /></div> : <img className=\"creation-media-preview-image\" src={url} alt=\"媒体预览\" /> : null}</Modal>;\r\n}\r\n\r\nfunction CreationAttachmentThumbnail({ item, onPreview, onRemove }: {\r\n    item: CreationAttachment;\r\n    onPreview: (type: \"image\" | \"video\" | \"audio\", url: string) => void;\r\n    onRemove: (id: string) => void;\r\n}) {\r\n    const kind = creationAttachmentKind(item);\r\n    const previewable = kind !== \"file\";\r\n    const url = (kind === \"video\" || kind === \"audio\" ? item.url : item.previewUrl) || \"\";\r\n    return <div className=\"creation-reference-card-content\">\r\n        {kind === \"file\" ? <a className=\"creation-reference-card-preview is-file\" href={item.url} download={item.name} aria-label={`下载原始附件 ${item.name}`}><span className=\"creation-chat-file-icon\"><FileText /><em>{item.name}</em></span></a> : <button type=\"button\" className={`creation-reference-card-preview${kind === \"audio\" ? \" is-file\" : \"\"}`} onClick={() => onPreview(kind, url)} aria-label={`${kind === \"audio\" ? \"试听\" : \"放大预览\"} ${item.name}`} disabled={!url}>\r\n            {kind === \"video\" ? <video src={item.url} poster={item.previewUrl !== item.url ? item.previewUrl : undefined} muted playsInline preload=\"metadata\" aria-label={item.name} /> : kind === \"image\" ? <img src={item.previewUrl} alt={item.name} /> : <span className=\"creation-chat-file-icon\">{kind === \"audio\" ? <Music2 /> : <FileText />}<em>{item.name}</em></span>}\r\n            {previewable ? <span aria-hidden=\"true\"><Maximize2 /></span> : null}\r\n        </button>}\r\n        <button type=\"button\" className=\"creation-reference-card-remove\" onClick={(event) => { event.stopPropagation(); onRemove(item.id); }} aria-label={`移除 ${item.name}`}><X /></button>\r\n    </div>;\r\n}\r\n\r\ntype ComposerProps = {\r\n    variant: \"empty\" | \"thread\" | \"lobby\";\r\n    agentIntent: boolean;\r\n    onAgentIntentChange: () => void;\r\n    confirmTools: boolean;\r\n    onConfirmToolsChange: (value: boolean) => void;\r\n    onSelectSkill: (skill: Skill) => Promise<void>;\r\n    onAgentModelChange: (kind: \"text\" | \"image\" | \"video\", value: string) => void;\r\n    uploading: boolean;\r\n    uploadFailures: { file: File; error: string }[];\r\n    submissionBlocked: boolean;\r\n    inputIssues: string[];\r\n    onAddFiles: (files: File[]) => void;\r\n    onRetryUploads: () => void;\r\n    onDismissUploadFailure: (file: File) => void;\r\n    mode: CreationMode;\r\n    prompt: string;\r\n    setPrompt: (value: string) => void;\r\n    busy: boolean;\r\n    attachments: CreationAttachment[];\r\n    referenceImageSize?: { width: number; height: string) => void;\r\n    quality: string;\r\n    setQuality: (value: string) => void;\r\n    videoQuality: string;\r\n    setVideoQuality: (value: string) => void;\r\n    count: string;\r\n    setCount: (value: string) => void;\r\n    promptOptimizerProvider: PromptOptimizerProvider | null;\r\n    composerFocusRef: RefObject<HTMLTextAreaElement | null>;\r\n    placeholderOverride?: string;\r\n    onSubmit: () => void;\r\n};\r\n\r\nfunction CreationComposer(props: ComposerProps) {\r\n    const [draggingFiles, setDraggingFiles] = useState(false);\r\n    const [lobbyExpanded, setLobbyExpanded] = useState(false);\r\n    const [previewUrl, setPreviewUrl] = useState(\"\");\r\n    const [previewType, setPreviewType] = useState<\"image\" | \"video\" | \"audio\">(\"image\");\r\n    const [promptOptimizerOpen, setPromptOptimizerOpen] = useState(false);\r\n    const attachmentTrackRef = useRef<HTMLUListElement>(null);\r\n    const cardDragRef = useRef<{ startX: number; startY: number; moved: boolean } | null>(null);\r\n    const suppressAttachmentClickRef = useRef(false);\r\n    const [trackState, setTrackState] = useState({ canScrollLeft: false, canScrollRight: false, isExpanded: false, isDragging: false });\r\n    const canSubmit = Boolean(props.prompt.trim()) && !props.busy && !props.submissionBlocked;\r\n    const creditsEnabled = useUserStore((state) => state.features.creditsEnabled);\r\n    const priceChannel = resolveModelChannel(props.config, props.model);\r\n    const canOptimizePrompt = !props.agentIntent && Boolean(props.promptOptimizerProvider) && (props.mode === \"image\" || props.mode === \"video\");\r\n    const optimizerReferences = props.references.filter((reference) => reference.active && reference.kind !== \"skill\");\r\n    const credits = requestCreditCost({\r\n        channelMode: priceChannel.scope === \"system\" ? \"remote\" : \"local\",\r\n        modelCosts: priceChannel.modelCosts,\r\n        model: modelOptionName(props.model),\r\n        count: props.mode === \"image\" ? props.count : 1,\r\n        seconds: props.mode === \"video\" ? props.seconds : 1,\r\n    });\r\n    const showCost = props.variant !== \"lobby\" && !props.agentIntent && creditsEnabled && credits !== null;\r\n    const formattedCredits = credits?.toLocaleString(\"zh-CN\", { maximumFractionDigits: 6 });\r\n    const actionLabel = props.busy ? \"正在处理\" : props.uploading ? \"附件处理中\" : props.variant === \"lobby\" ? \"进入映雪画布\" : props.agentIntent ? \"带入画布 Agent\" : showCost ? \"描述画面、人物、场景、构图与风格\"\r\n            : \"描述镜头内容、运动、光线与节奏\";\r\n    const emptyPlaceholder = \"输入你的镜头、画面或故事。也可以添加参考图开始创作\";\r\n    const imageReferencesSupported = props.imageProfile.references.maxImages > 0;\r\n    const referencesSupported = props.agentIntent || (props.mode === \"image\" ? imageReferencesSupported : props.mode !== \"video\" || props.videoProfile.references.maxImages + props.videoProfile.references.maxVideos + props.videoProfile.references.maxAudios > 0);\r\n    const canAddMoreReferences = referencesSupported && props.attachments.length < props.maxReferences;\r\n    const imageSettingsSupported = props.imageProfile.size.parameter !== \"none\" || props.imageProfile.quality.supported || props.imageProfile.maxOutputs > 1;\r\n    const updateTrackScrollState = useCallback(() => {\r\n        const track = attachmentTrackRef.current;\r\n        if (!track) return;\r\n        setTrackState((current) => ({\r\n            ...current,\r\n            canScrollLeft: track.scrollLeft > 1,\r\n            canScrollRight: track.scrollLeft + track.clientWidth < track.scrollWidth - 1,\r\n        }));\r\n    }, []);\r\n    const handleTrackMouseEnter = useCallback(() => {\r\n        if (window.matchMedia(\"(hover: hover)\").matches) setTrackState((current) => ({ ...current, isExpanded: true }));\r\n    }, []);\r\n    const handleTrackMouseLeave = useCallback(() => {\r\n        if (!trackState.isDragging) setTrackState((current) => ({ ...current, isExpanded: false }));\r\n    }, [trackState.isDragging]);\r\n    const handleTrackFocus = useCallback(() => {\r\n        setTrackState((current) => ({ ...current, isExpanded: true }));\r\n    }, []);\r\n    const handleTrackBlur = useCallback((event: FocusEvent<HTMLElement>) => {\r\n        if (!trackState.isDragging && !event.currentTarget.contains(event.relatedTarget as Node | null)) {\r\n            setTrackState((current) => ({ ...current, isExpanded: false }));\r\n        }\r\n    }, [trackState.isDragging]);\r\n    useEffect(() => {\r\n        const touchOnly = window.matchMedia(\"(hover: none)\").matches;\r\n        setTrackState((current) => ({ ...current, isExpanded: touchOnly || current.isDragging }));\r\n        updateTrackScrollState();\r\n    }, [props.attachments.length, updateTrackScrollState]);\r\n    useEffect(() => {\r\n        const frame = window.requestAnimationFrame(updateTrackScrollState);\r\n        return () => window.cancelAnimationFrame(frame);\r\n    }, [trackState.isExpanded, updateTrackScrollState]);\r\n    const beginCardDrag = (event: PointerEvent<HTMLElement>) => {\r\n        if (event.button !== 0 || props.busy) return;\r\n        if ((event.target as HTMLElement).closest(\".creation-reference-card-remove\")) return;\r\n        cardDragRef.current = { startX: event.clientX, startY: event.clientY, moved: false };\r\n    };\r\n    const endCardDrag = (event: PointerEvent<HTMLElement>) => {\r\n        const drag = cardDragRef.current;\r\n        if (!drag) return;\r\n        cardDragRef.current = null;\r\n        if (drag.moved) {\r\n            suppressAttachmentClickRef.current = true;\r\n            window.setTimeout(() => { suppressAttachmentClickRef.current = false; }, 0);\r\n        }\r\n        if (event.currentTarget.hasPointerCapture(event.pointerId)) event.currentTarget.releasePointerCapture(event.pointerId);\r\n        setTrackState((current) => ({ ...current, isDragging: false }));\r\n    };\r\n    const moveCardDrag = (event: PointerEvent<HTMLElement>) => {\r\n        const drag = cardDragRef.current;\r\n        if (!drag || drag.moved) return;\r\n        if (Math.hypot(event.clientX - drag.startX, event.clientY - drag.startY) <= 4) return;\r\n        drag.moved = true;\r\n        setTrackState((current) => ({ ...current, isDragging: true, isExpanded: true }));\r\n    };\r\n    const previewAttachment = (type: \"image\" | \"video\" | \"audio\", url: string) => {\r\n        if (suppressAttachmentClickRef.current || cardDragRef.current?.moved) return;\r\n        setPreviewType(type);\r\n        setPreviewUrl(url);\r\n    };\r\n    useEffect(() => {\r\n        if (!canOptimizePrompt) setPromptOptimizerOpen(false);\r\n    }, [canOptimizePrompt]);\r\n\r\n    const scrollAttachmentTrack = (direction: -1 | 1) => {\r\n        const track = attachmentTrackRef.current;\r\n        if (!track) return;\r\n        track.scrollBy({ left: direction * Math.max(track.clientWidth * 0.72, 120), behavior: \"smooth\" });\r\n        window.setTimeout(updateTrackScrollState, 180);\r\n    };\r\n    const lobbyIsExpanded = props.variant === \"lobby\" && (lobbyExpanded || Boolean(props.prompt.trim()) || props.attachments.length > 0 || draggingFiles);\r\n    const composer = <section className={`creation-chat-composer is-${props.variant} has-structured-input${draggingFiles ? \" is-file-dragging\" : \"\"}${lobbyIsExpanded ? \" is-expanded\" : \"\"}${props.attachments.length ? \" has-attachments\" : \"\"}`}\r\n        onPointerDown={event => {\r\n            if (props.variant !== \"lobby\" || !(event.target instanceof Element) || event.target.closest(\"button, a, input, textarea, [contenteditable='true'], [role='button']\")) return;\r\n            props.composerFocusRef.current?.focus();\r\n        }}\r\n        onDragOver={event => { if (event.dataTransfer.types.includes(\"Files\")) { event.preventDefault(); if (!props.busy) setDraggingFiles(true); } }}\r\n        onDragLeave={event => { if (!event.currentTarget.contains(event.relatedTarget as Node | null)) setDraggingFiles(false); }}\r\n        onDrop={event => { if (!event.dataTransfer.files.length) return; event.preventDefault(); setDraggingFiles(false); if (!props.busy) props.onAddFiles(Array.from(event.dataTransfer.files)); }}\r\n        onPaste={event => { if (event.clipboardData.files.length && !props.busy) { event.preventDefault(); props.onAddFiles(Array.from(event.clipboardData.files)); } }}>\r\n        {draggingFiles && <div className=\"creation-drop-hint\">松开以添加参考素材</div>}\r\n        <div className=\"creation-chat-writing-surface\">\r\n            <input ref={props.fileInputRef} type=\"file\" hidden accept={creationUploadAccept(props.agentIntent ? \"text\" : props.mode)} multiple onChange={props.onFileChange} />\r\n            <div className=\"creation-chat-editor\">\r\n                <CanvasResourceMentionTextarea ref={props.composerFocusRef} value={props.prompt} references={props.references} mentionMenuWidth={400} sendOnEnter={props.variant === \"lobby\"} onChange={props.setPrompt} onSubmit={props.onSubmit} onContentSizeChange={props.variant === \"lobby\" ? height => setLobbyExpanded(height > 48) : undefined} containerClassName=\"creation-chat-mention-container\" className=\"creation-chat-mention-editor creation-scrollbar\" style={{ color: \"var(--creation-text)\" }} placeholder={props.agentIntent ? \"说出你的创意，或者从一个 Skill 开始创作…\" : props.placeholderOverride || (props.variant === \"empty\" ? emptyPlaceholder : placeholder)} aria-label=\"创作提示词，可使用 @ 引用当前参考内容或技能\" spellCheck disabled={props.busy} />\r\n                {props.attachments.length || (props.variant !== \"lobby\" && canAddMoreReferences) ? <div className=\"creation-reference-track-wrapper\" inert={props.busy}>\r\n     {trackState.canScrollLeft ? <button type=\"button\" className=\"creation-reference-track-button is-left\" onClick={() => scrollAttachmentTrack(-1)} aria-label=\"向左浏览参考内容\"         ref={attachmentTrackRef}\r\n                            className={`creation-reference-track${trackState.isExpanded ? \" is-expanded\" : \"\"}${trackState.isDragging ? \" is-dragging\" :            className=\"creation-reference-stack-card\"\r\n                                onPointerDown={beginCardDrag}\r\n                                onPointerMove={moveCardDrag}\r\n                                onPointerUp={endCardDrag}\r\n                                onPointerCancel={endCardDrag}\r\n                                onDragStart={() => setTrackState((current) => ({                  >\r\n                                <CreationAttachmentThumbnail item={item} onPreview={previewAttachment} onRemove={props.onRemoveAttachment} />\r\n                            </Reorder.Item>)}\r\n                            {canAddMoreReferences ? <li className=\"creation-reference-add-slot\"><Tooltip title=\"添加更多参考内容\"><button type=\"button\"         </Reorder.Group>\r\n                        {trackState.canScrollRight ? <button type=\"button\" className=\"creation-reference-track-button is-right\" onClick={() => scrollAttachmentTrack(1)} </div>\r\n        {(props.uploading || props.uploadFailures.length > 0 || props.inputIssues.length > 0) && <div className=\"creation-input-status\" aria-live=\"polite\">\r\n            {props.uploading && <span><LoaderCircle size={14} className=\"animate-spin\" />正在处理附件，完成后即可提交</span>}\r\n            {props.inputIssues.map(issue => <p key={issue} role=\"alert\">{issue}</p>)}\r\n            {props.uploadFailures.map((item, index) => <p key={`${item.file.name}:${index}`} role=\"alert\"><span>{item.file.name}：{item.error}</span><button type=\"button\" onClick={() => onTranscribed={(text) => props.setPrompt(props.prompt.trim() ? `${props.prompt} ${text}` : text)}\r\n                /> : null}\r\n                <ModePicker mode={props.mode}           type=\"button\"\r\n                        className=\"creation-chat-control\"\r\n                        onClick={() => setPromptOptimizerOpen(true)}\r\n 执行方式：${props.confirmTools ? \"逐步确认\" : \"自动执行\"}`} title=\"选择 Agent 执行方式\"><SlidersHorizontal /><span>{props.confirmTools ? \"逐步确认\" : \"自动执行\"}</span><ChevronDown /></button></Popover>\r\n                </> : props.variant !== \"lobby\" ? <>\r\n                    {props.mode === \"video\" || (props.mode === \"image\" && imageSettingsSupported) ? <GenerationSettingsMenu {...props} /> : null}\r\n                    {props.mode === \"video\" ? <DurationMenu profile={props.videoProfile} seconds={props.seconds} onChange={props.setSeconds} /> : null}\r\n                </> : null}\r\n \"--canvas-composer-submit-action\": !props.busy && !canSubmit ? \"var(--creation-surface-hover)\" : \"var(--creation-text)\",\r\n                    \"--canvas-composer-submit-action-fg\": !props.busy && !canSubmit ? \"var(--creation-faint)\" : \"var(--creation-bg)\",\r\n                } as CSSProperties}\r\n                onClick={props.busy ? undefined : props.onSubmit}\r\n                aria-label={actionLabel}\r\n                title={actionLabel}\r\n            >\r\n                {showCost ? <span className=\"canvas-node-composer-submit-cost\"><CreditSymbol generationMode={props.mode === \"video\" ? \"video\" : \"image\"}\r\n            targetModel={modelOptionName(props.model) || props.model}\r\n            targetProtocol={priceChannel.modelCosts?.find((item) => item.model === modelOptionName(props.model))?.protocol || priceChannel.interfaceType}\r\n            config={props.config}\r\n            optimizerModel={props.config.textModel}\r\n            references={optimizerReferences}\r\n            provider={props.promptOptimizerProvider}\r\n            onClose={() => /></button></Popover>;\r\n}\r\n\r\nfunction GenerationSettingsMenu(props: ComposerProps) {\r\n    const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);\r\n    const [customRatioOpen, setCustomRatioOpen] = useState(!ratioOptions.some((option) => option.value === props.ratio));\r\n    const activeQualityOptions = props.imageProfile.quality.values.map((value) => qualityOptions.find((item) => item.value === value) || { value, label: value.toUpperCase(), description: \"模型支持的质量/分辨率\" });\r\n    const qualityLabel = activeQualityOptions.find((item) => item.value === props.quality)?.label || qualityOptions.find((item) => item.value === props.quality)?.label || props.quality || \"自动\";\r\n    // 尺寸/比例/分辨率选项取同显示名分组内全部模型的并集，路由模型只决定发送参数。\r\n    const mergedProfile = mergedImageCapabilityConfig(props.config, props.model || props.config.imageModel);\r\n    const usesImageResolutionPicker = props.mode === \"image\" && supportsImageResolutionPresets(mergedProfile.size);\r\n    const imageResolutionOptions = usesImageResolutionPicker ? buildImageResolutionOptions(mergedProfile.size.values) : [];\r\n    const   const imageResolutionChoiceOptions = usesImageResolutionPicker ? imageResolutionChoices(mergedProfile.size.values) : [];\r\n    const imageRatios = usesImageResolutionPicker\r\n        ? String(referenceImageSize.width) + \"x\" + String(referenceImageSize.height) : \"\";\r\n    const referenceImageSizeLabel = referenceImageSize ? String(referenceImageSize.width) + \" × \" + String(referenceImageSize.height) : \"\";\r\n    const referenceImageSizeRatio = referenceImageSize ? String(referenceImageSize.width) + \":\" + String(referenceImageSize.height) : \"\";\r\n    const referenceImageSizeSelected = Boolean(referenceImageSizeValue && props.ratio === referenceImageSizeValue);\r\n    const resolutions = props.mode === \"video\" ? props.videoProfile.resolutions.map((value) => ({ value: value.replace(/p$/i, \"\"), label: videoResolutionLabel(value) })) : resolutionOptions;\r\n    const selectImageRatio = (nextRatio: string) => {\r\n        if (!usesImageResolutionPicker || activeImageResolutionChoice === \"auto\") {\r\n            props.setRatio(nextRatio);\r\n            return;\r\n        }\r\n        props.setRatio(imageSizeForResolution(imageResolutionOptions, activeImageResolutionChoice, nextRatio) || nextRatio);\r\n    };\r\n    const selectImageResolution = (choice: ImageResolutionChoice) => {\r\n        if (choice === \"auto\") {\r\n            props.setRatio(mergedProfile.size.values.includes(\"auto\") ? \"auto\" : activeImageRatio);\r\n            return;\r\n        }\r\n        const nextSize = imageSizeForResolution(imageResolutionOptions, choice, activeImageRatio) || imageResolutionOptions.find((item) => item.tier === choice)?.size;\r\n        if (nextSize) props.setRatio(nextSize);\r\n    };\r\n    const selectReferenceImageSize = () => {\r\n        if (!referenceImageSizeValue) return;\r\n        props.setRatio(referenceImageSizeValue);\r\n        setCustomRatioOpen(false);\r\n    };\r\n    const videoResolutionSupported = props.mode === \"video\" && resolutions.length > 0;\r\n    const imageSummary = [\r\n        ...(mergedProfile.size.parameter !== \"none\" ? [referenceImageSizeSelected ? referenceImageSizeLabel : usesImageResolutionPicker ? formatImageResolutionSize(props.ratio, imageResolutionOptions) : props.ratio] : []),\r\n        ...(props.imageProfile.quality.supported ? [qualityLabel] : []),\r\n        ...(props.imageProfile.maxOutputs > 1 ? [props.count] : []),\r\n    ].join(\" · \");\r\n    const summary = props.mode === \"video\" ? [props.ratio, ...(videoResolutionSupported ? [videoResolutionLabel(props.videoQuality)] : [])].join(\" · \") : imageSummary;\r\n    const panel = <div className=\"creation-parameter-menu\">\r\n        {props.mode === \"video\" || mergedProfile.size.parameter !== \"none\" ? value === activeImageRatio : value === props.ratio; return <button key={value} type=\"button\" aria-pressed={selected} className={selected ? \"is-selected\" : \"\"} onClick={() => { if (props.mode === \"image\") selectImageRatio(value); else props.setRatio(value); setCustomRatioOpen(false); }}><span className=\"creation-ratio-preview\"><span style={ratioPreviewStyle(value)} /></span><span>{value}</span></button>; })}</div>{props.mode !== \"video\" && mergedProfile.size.allowCustom && (customRatioOpen ? <label className=\"creation-custom-value\"><span>宽 x 高</span><input value={props.ratio} onFocus={(event) => event.currentTarget.select()} onChange={(event) => props.setRatio(event.target.value)} placeholder=\"1920x1080 或 2:1\" aria-label=\"自定义图片尺寸或比例\" /></label> : <button type=\"button\" className=\"creation-custom-trigger\" onClick={() => setCustomRatioOpen(true)}><Plus />输入自定义尺寸</button>)}</div></SettingSection> : null}\r\n        {props.mode === \"video\" ? (videoResolutionSupported ? <SettingSection title=\"清晰度\" value={videoResolutionLabel(props.videoQuality)}><div className=\"creation-choice-grid is-resolution\">{resolutions.map((option) => <button key={option.value} type=\"button\" aria-pressed={option.value === props.videoQuality} className={option.value === props.videoQuality ? \"is-selected\" : \"\"} onClick={() => props.setVideoQuality(option.value)}>{option.label}</button>)}</div></SettingSection> : null) : <>\r\n            {imageResolutionChoiceOptions.length ? <SettingSection title=\"分辨率\" value={activeImageResolutionChoice === \"auto\" ? \"自动\" : activeImageResolutionChoice.toUpperCase()}><div className=\"creation-choice-grid is-resolution\">{imageResolutionChoiceOptions.map((choice) => <button key={choice} type=\"button\" aria-pressed={choice === activeImageResolutionChoice} className={choice === activeImageResolutionChoice ? \"is-selected\" : \"\"} onClick={() => selectImageResolution(choice)}>{choice === \"auto\" ? \"自动\" : choice.toUpperCase()}</button>)}</div></SettingSection> : null}\r\n            {props.imageProfile.quality.supported ? <SettingSection title={activeQualityOptions.some((item) => item.value === \"1k\" || item.value === \"2k\") ? \"分辨率\" : \"图片质量\"} value={qualityLabel}><div className=\"creation-choice-grid is-quality\">{activeQualityOptions.map((option) => <button key={option.value} type=\"button\" aria-pressed={option.value === props.quality} className={option.value === props.quality ? \"is-open\" : \"\"} /></button>\r\n    </Popover>;\r\n}\r\n\r\nfunction SettingSection({ title, value, children }: { title: string; value?: string; children: ReactNode }) {\r\n    return <section className=\"creation-parameter-section\"><header><h3>{title}</h3>{value ? <span>{value}</span> : null}</header>{children}</section>;\r\n}\r\n\r\nfunction DurationMenu({ profile, seconds, onChange }: { profile: VideoCapabilityConfig; seconds: string; onChange: (value: string) => void }) {\r\n    const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);\r\n    const value = Number(normalizeVideoValue(profile, { seconds }).seconds);\r\n    const presets = profile.duration.selection === \"enum\" ? </div></div>;\r\n    }\r\n    if (status === \"error\") return <div className=\"storyboard-workbench-error\"><span>{generationErrorMessage(result.error || \"\")}</span><button type=\"button\" />\r\n    </>;\r\n}\r\n\r\nfunction videoResolutionLabel(value: string | number) {\r\n    return Number(String(value).replace(/p$/i, \"\")) === 2160 ? \"4K\" : `${String(value).replace(/p$/i, \"\")}P`;\r\n}\r\n\r\nfunction formatMessageTime(value: string) {\r\n    const timestamp = new Date(value).getTime();\r\n    return Number.isFinite(timestamp) ? conversationTimeFormatter.format(timestamp) : \"\";\r\n}\r\n\r\nfunction conversationPreviewMessage(conversation: CreationConversation) {\r\n    let fallback: CreationMessage | undefined;\r\n    for (let index = conversation.messages.length - 1; index >= 0; index -= 1) {\r\n        const message = conversation.messages[index];\r\n        if (!message.content.trim()) continue;\r\n        fallback ||= message;\r\n        if (message.role === \"user\") return message;\r\n    }\r\n    return fallback;\r\n}\r\n\r\nasync function buildTextMessageContent(item: CreationMessage) {\r\n    const content = expandCreationPrompt(item.content, item.references || [], item.attachments || []);\r\n    const attachments = item.attachments || [];\r\n    if (!attachments.length) return content;\r\n    const parts: AiTextContentPart[] = [{ type: \"text\", text: content }];\r\n    for (const attachment of attachments) {\r\n        if (isImageAttachment(attachment)) {\r\n            parts.push({ type: \"image_url\", image_url: { url: attachment.dataUrl || attachment.url || \"\" } });\r\n            continue;\r\n        }\r\n        const url = await creationAttachmentDataUrl(attachment);\r\n        parts.push({ type: \"file_url\", file_url: { url, name: attachment.name || \"附件\", mimeType: attachment.type || \"application/octet-stream\" } });\r\n    }\r\n    return parts;\r\n}\r\n\r\nasync function creationAttachmentDataUrl(attachment: CreationAttachment) {\r\n    if ((attachment.bytes || 0) > TEXT_ATTACHMENT_MAX_BYTES) throw new Error(`${attachment.name} 超过 20MB，当前文本模型附件需要压缩后再上传`);\r\n    const attachmentUrl = attachment.url || \"\";\r\n    if (attachmentUrl.startsWith(\"data:\")) return attachmentUrl;\r\n    const blob = attachment.storageKey ? await getMediaBlob(attachment.storageKey) : null;\r\n    if (blob) {\r\n        if (blob.size > TEXT_ATTACHMENT_MAX_BYTES) throw new Error(`${attachment.name} 超过 20MB，当前文本模型附件需要压缩后再上传`);\r\n        return blobToDataUrl(blob);\r\n    }\r\n    if (/^https:\\/\\//i.test(attachmentUrl)) return attachmentUrl;\r\n    throw new Error(`${attachment.name} 无法读取，请重新上传后再试`);\r\n}\r\n\r\nfunction blobToDataUrl(blob: Blob) {\r\n    return new Promise<string>((resolve, reject) => {\r\n        const reader = new FileReader();\r\n        reader.onload = () => resolve(String(reader.result || \"\"));\r\n        reader.onerror = () => reject(reader.error || new Error(\"附件读取失败\"));\r\n        reader.readAsDataURL(blob);\r\n    });\r\n}\r\n\r\nfunction isVideoAttachment(attachment: CreationAttachment): attachment is CreationAttachment & { url: string } {\r\n    return creationAttachmentKind(attachment) === \"video\";\r\n}\r\n\r\nfunction isImageAttachment(attachment: CreationAttachment): attachment is CreationAttachment & { dataUrl: string; width?: number; height?: number } {\r\n    return creationAttachmentKind(attachment) === \"image\";\r\n}\r\n\r\ntype PersistedCreationTask = GenerationTask & { creationResultUrls?: string[]; creationError?: string };\r\n\r\nfunction attachCreationTaskContexts(tasks: GenerationTask[], conversations: CreationConversation[]) {\r\n    const contexts = new Map<string, { prompt: string; clientContext: NonNullable<GenerationTask[\"clientContext\"]> }>();\r\n    for (const conversation of conversations) {\r\n        for (const [messageIndex, message] of conversation.messages.entries()) {\r\n            if (message.role !== \"assistant\" || !message.taskIds?.length) continue;\r\n            const prompt = conversation.messages[messageIndex - 1]?.role === \"user\" ? conversation.messages[messageIndex - 1].content : \"\";\r\n            for (const [batchIndex, taskId] of message.taskIds.entries()) contexts.set(taskId, { prompt, clientContext: { conversationId: conversation.id, messageId: message.id, batchIndex, batchCount: message.taskIds.length } });\r\n        }\r\n    }\r\n    return tasks.map((task) => {\r\n        const context = contexts.get(task.id);\r\n        return context ? { ...task, prompt: context.prompt, clientContext: context.clientContext } : task;\r\n    });\r\n}\r\n\r\nasync function materializeCreationTaskResults(tasks: GenerationTask[], signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<PersistedCreationTask[]> {\r\n    return Promise.all(tasks.map(async (task): Promise<PersistedCreationTask> => {\r\n        // 文本正文保存在 resultJson，不进入媒体资源化链路。\r\n        if (task.status !== \"succeeded\" || !task.clientContext || task.type === \"canvas_text\") return task;\r\n        try {\r\n            const materialized = await runGenerationConsumer(signal, (managedSignal) => materializeGenerationTaskAssets(task, managedSignal));\r\n            const creationResultUrls = generationTaskMaterializedUrls(materialized);\r\n            return creationResultUrls.length ? { ...materialized, creationResultUrls } : materialized;\r\n        } catch (error) {\r\n            return { ...task, creationError: error instanceof Error ? error.message : \"生成结果资源化失败\" };\r\n        }\r\n    }));\r\n}\r\n\r\nfunction reconcileCreationTaskMessages(conversations: CreationConversation[], tasks: PersistedCreationTask[]) {\r\n    let changed = false;\r\n    const next = conversations.map((conversation) => {\r\n        let conversationChanged = false;\r\n        let completedAt = conversation.updatedAt;\r\n        const messages = conversation.messages.map((message) => {\r\n            const taskIds = new Set(message.taskIds || []);\r\n            const matches = tasks\r\n                .filter((task) => taskIds.has(task.id) || (task.clientContext?.conversationId === conversation.id && task.clientContext.messageId === message.id))\r\n                .sort((left, right) => (left.clientContext?.batchIndex || 0) - (right.clientContext?.batchIndex || 0));\r\n            if (message.role === \"assistant\" && message.mode === \"text\") {\r\n conversationTimestamp(task.updatedAt) > conversationTimestamp(latest) ? task.updatedAt : latest, completedAt);\r\n                conversationChanged = true;\r\n                changed = true;\r\n                return { ...message, ...recovery };\r\n            }\r\n            if (message.role !== \"assistant\" || message.status !== \"pending\") return message;\r\n            const expectedTaskCount = Math.max(0, ...matches.map((task) => task.clientContext?.batchCount || 0));\r\n            if (!matches.length || (expectedTaskCount > 0 && matches.length < expectedTaskCount) || \"succeeded\").flatMap(creationTaskResultUrls)));\r\n            const failedCount = matches.filter((task) => task.status !== \"succeeded\" || Boolean(task.creationError)).length;\r\n            const nextTaskIds = Array.from(new Set([...(message.taskIds || []), ...matches.map((task) => task.id)]));\r\n            completedAt = matches.reduce((latest, task) => conversationTimestamp(task.updatedAt) > conversationTimestamp(latest) ? task.updatedAt : latest, completedAt);\r\n            conversationChanged = true;\r\n            changed = true;\r\n\r\n            if (resultUrls.length) {\r\n                const content = message.mode === \"video\" ? \"视频已生成\" : failedCount ? `${resultUrls.length} 张图片已生成，${failedCount} 张失败` : \"图片已生成\";\r\n                return { ...message, status: \"done\" as const, content, resultUrls, error: undefined, taskIds: nextTaskIds };\r\n            }\r\n            if (matches.every((task) => task.status === \"cancelled\")) {\r\n                const ...message, status: \"error\" as const, content: \"生成失败\", error: generationErrorMessage(failed?.creationError || failed?.error || \"任务已结束，但生成结果暂时无法读取\"), taskIds: nextTaskIds };\r\n        });\r\n        return conversationChanged ? { ...conversation, messages, updatedAt: completedAt } : conversation;\r\n    });\r\n    return changed ? next : conversations;\r\n}\r\n\r\nfunction height: 10 };\r\n    // 画幅容器的可用空间是 14×10；同时计算宽高，避免 CSS 的 max-width/max-height 把宽银幕比例压扁。\r\n    const scale = Math.min(14 / width, 10 / height);\r\n    return { width: Math.max(4, Math.round(width *
